Router matches the user's problem description against recipe keywords and returns the best fit. - **sci-paper-cn**: 按 CVPR、ICCV、NeurIPS、ICML、ACL、ICLR 等顶级会议惯例撰写、排版与呈现科研论文的结构化指南。覆盖全流程：分章节起草、图表设计、LaTeX 排版、叙事逻辑与投稿前打磨。适用于撰写、修改或将手稿排版成 camera-ready 或预印本 PDF。 ([haomingz/kimi-skills](https://github.com/haomingz/kimi-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-paper-cn) · ★25 · 5 copies)

- **sci-paper-writing**: 地理学、遥感、土壤、生态方向 SCI 英文研究论文的写作与修改。覆盖三类研究论文：时空变化（趋势、变化检测、驱动归因）、定量反演与模拟（土壤盐分、SOC、土壤水分、植被参数等）、观测对照（处理与对照、野外实验）。对标新锐分区地理学相关 1 区期刊（RSE、ISPRS J、TGRS、JAG、IJGIS、Catena、Geoderma、Ecological Indicators 等）。用于选题与创新点判断、选刊、标题、摘要、Highlights、引言、研究区、数据与方法、精度验证、结果、讨论、结论、图件、去 AI 味润色、cover letter 与投稿自查。用户提到写/改/润色/审查地理或遥感论文的任何部分、问"创新点够不够""投哪本刊""引言怎么写""讨论怎么写""SHAP 怎么写""怎么去 AI 味"时使用。综述与 Meta 分析用 geo-review-writing；回复审稿意见用 geo-reviewer-response；建立个人写作风格档案用 geo-writing-style。 ([Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors](https://github.com/Xiangyu-Ge/sci-writing-geors/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-paper-writing) · ★62)
- **sci-papers-downloder**: Search academic papers (OpenAlex no-key by default, or Scopus with a built-in key) and download full-text PDFs concurrently — OA direct link → Unpaywall → Sci-Hub fallback (on by default). - **sci-peer-review**: 只读审查科研论文或研究方案，依据实际材料分析主张、方法、实验、统计、引用和可复现性，输出有位置和修正建议的意见；不代替期刊审稿或修改原稿。 ([AIM135D/sci-research-workflow](https://github.com/AIM135D/sci-research-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-peer-review))
- **sci-planner**: Create a verified, actionable plan for a task by spawning a planner agent and a plan-checker agent with research context. Use this skill when the user asks to 'plan this', 'create a plan for', 'design an experiment', 'make a plan', 'plan out how to', or any request that involves creating a structured, step-by-step plan before execution. Also trigger when the user says 'deep plan' or 'plan with verification'. Do NOT trigger for simple todo lists or when the user just wants to talk through an approach casually. ([JakeWeinstein/SciAi](https://github.com/JakeWeinstein/SciAi/tree/HEAD/sci-planner) · ★1)
- **sci-plot**: Scientific figure prompt engine. Analyzes papers/projects/algorithms/code/documents, matches against a dual-track rules pool (local & cloud/external skills), applies the Scope Isolation ("烟消云散") rule, and generates high-quality, cross-platform image generation prompts. Users copy prompts into ChatGPT/DALL-E, Doubao, Qwen, Midjourney, Stable Diffusion, or use them as guides for SVG/Matlab/Origin. Does not generate images directly — produces prompts only for maximum flexibility and environmental independence. ([LPK3215/sci-plot](https://github.com/LPK3215/sci-plot/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★3)
- **sci-polish**: Two-stage academic paper polishing skill. Stage A reduces AI detection traces (targeting GPTZero, Turnitin, Originality.ai). Stage B performs 8-dimension quality improvement (grammar, tone, coherence, conciseness, terminology, structure, argument clarity, journal compliance). Use whenever the user asks to polish, proofread, humanize, reduce AI rate, improve writing quality, or prepare a paper for submission. ([ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write](https://github.com/ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-polish) · ★225)

- **sci-reading-bench**: 把 /input 科研材料现场构建成可判分、可复核、可复跑的文献阅读题集与评测工具包；Agent 负责真实阅读、核验与撰写判断，脚本负责材料索引、逐字证据核验、0/1/2 评分、报告生成与 result.json 契约双回显。适用于科研阅读 Benchmark 建设、题集质量自检、材料问答/提取/核验的证据回溯。 ([Richard-He-happy/bohr-p4-portfolio](https://github.com/Richard-He-happy/bohr-p4-portfolio/tree/HEAD/challenges/reading-11))
- **sci-rebuttal**: Use after Science reviews arrive to triage the decision, prioritize experiments, and draft a point-by-point response that is respectful, evidence-led, and honest about limits. Do not run before the main text is actually revised. ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Science-Skills/skills/sci-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **sci-reference**: Use when checking a numerical anti-pattern, weighing a contested question, confirming whether a tooling or ecosystem claim is still current (snapshot verified August 2026), finding the books, documentation and people, or needing the numbers to hold, the what-to-check list for when the numbers are wrong, and a method picker. Companion to the other math-science-programming skills. ([the-vibey-project/vibey](https://github.com/the-vibey-project/vibey/tree/HEAD/src/vibey_tools/skills/plugins/math-science-programming/skills/sci-reference) · ★5)
- **sci-reference-audit**: SCI级文献真实性与引用一致性审计工具。对学术论文参考文献列表进行系统性联网核查， 验证文献真实性、DOI有效性、作者/标题/期刊/年份/卷期页准确性， 识别AI伪造文献，生成10节标准Markdown审计报告。 核心特色：多轮次验证、多数据源交叉、多Agent并行互查、审计后自学习。 触发词：审计文献、文献真实性、reference audit、查文献、验证引用、核查参考文献、 文献核实、文献校验、假文献检测、SCI文献审计 ([BoycePeng/sci-reference-audit](https://github.com/BoycePeng/sci-reference-audit/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★1)
- **sci-report**: What an experiment report is: the exploratory and preregistered shapes, their templates and exemplars, the writing conventions, the lede, and the notes that carry review decisions between rounds. Read before drafting or reviewing a report or a plan. ([z0u/sca2](https://github.com/z0u/sca2/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-report) · ★1)
- **sci-research**: Conduct deep parallel research on a topic using multiple specialized agents (web search, scientific literature, and codebase analysis), then synthesize findings into a comprehensive document. This is an intensive research workflow that spawns multiple agents and produces thorough, detailed output — use it ONLY when the user explicitly asks for deep or thorough research. Trigger phrases: 'deep research', 'do a deep dive on', 'thorough literature review', 'comprehensive research on', 'deep dive into'. Do NOT trigger on casual research requests like 'look this up', 'search for', 'research this'… ([JakeWeinstein/SciAi](https://github.com/JakeWeinstein/SciAi/tree/HEAD/sci-research) · ★1)
- **sci-research-discussion**: Use when the project reaches 20-SCI研究Discussion撰写器 in the SCI original research workflow. 适用于临床、回顾性、生信、实验四条路线。 ([Richard0901/science-skills](https://github.com/Richard0901/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/20-SCI%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6Discussion%E6%92%B0%E5%86%99%E5%99%A8))
- **sci-research-introduction-writer**: Use when the project reaches the Introduction writing step in any of the 4 biomedical research routes (clinical/retrospective/bioinformatics/experimental). Writes Introduction based on research background, knowledge gaps, and scientific hypotheses. ([Richard0901/science-skills](https://github.com/Richard0901/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/22-SCI%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6Introduction%E6%92%B0%E5%86%99%E5%99%A8))
- **sci-research-mgmt**: Capture research notes, track projects with milestones and deadlines, schedule automated science tasks, and run cross-skill pipelines. Four modes via natural language: note (capture, search, promote), project (create, dashboard, detail, update), schedule (presets, custom, list), pipeline (run, create, list). Triggers on: "research note", "log observation", "jot down", "capture idea", "research project", "track project", "milestones", "deadlines", "project dashboard", "schedule alerts", "paper alerts", "set up monitoring", "deadline reminders", "run pipeline", "literature monitor", "data watch… ([krmdel/organon](https://github.com/krmdel/organon/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-research-mgmt) · ★8)
- **sci-research-paper-controller**: Use when the project reaches 00-SCI原创研究论文总控专家 in the SCI original research dynamic route workflow. ([Richard0901/science-skills](https://github.com/Richard0901/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/00-SCI%E5%8E%9F%E5%88%9B%E7%A0%94%E7%A9%B6%E8%AE%BA%E6%96%87%E6%80%BB%E6%8E%A7%E4%B8%93%E5%AE%B6))
- **sci-research-profile**: Build and manage a scientist's research profile via interactive onboarding. Captures core identity, research focus, preferences, and tool ecosystem. Stored in research_context/research-profile.md for downstream skills. Triggers on: "research profile", "set up my profile", "who am I", "my research", "update profile", "research onboarding", "configure research". Foundation skill -- run before any execution skill that reads research context. Does NOT trigger for literature search, data analysis, or manuscript writing. ([krmdel/organon](https://github.com/krmdel/organon/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sci-research-profile) · ★8)
- **sci-research-project-initializer**: Use when the project reaches 01-SCI研究项目初始化 in the SCI original research dynamic route workflow. - **sci-research-refine**: 在 SCI 创新筛选中细化候选研究机制的单 Agent 阶段，保持问题锚点和证据边界。 ([AIM135D/sci-research-workflow](https://github.com/AIM135D/sci-research-workflow/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/sci-research-refine))
- **sci-retr**: Sci Retriever(sci-retr) — DOI 목록이나 첨부 엑셀·WoS·Scopus 검색 결과의 논문 본문 PDF, 본문 텍스트, SI 를 수집한다. 논문 PDF·링크의 reference(참고문헌) 논문도 수집한다. "논문 받아줘", "엑셀 논문 리스트 수집해줘", "reference들 모두 수집해줘", "DOI 수집", "원문 다운로드", "SI 저장", "sci-retr 사용법"에 사용. 앞에 리버·김리버·김리버씨·댕댕아·댕댕이·김댕댕·김댕댕씨 호칭이 붙어도 수집 요청으로 인식한다. 수집 뒤 sci-index 색인은 항상 만들고, 한국어 한 줄 요약은 원할 때만 sci-tldr를 쓴다. ([angmond1/sci](https://github.com/angmond1/sci/tree/HEAD/sci-retr))

- **sci-review**: Specialized workflows for drafting, refining, and responding to academic literature reviews and peer review feedback. Use this skill for literature review outlines, research-gap synthesis, reviewer rebuttals, response letters, and academic writing tone repair. ([ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write](https://github.com/ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review) · ★225)
- **sci-review-benchmark**: 找 3–8 篇 标杆综述（同主题/同方法/顶刊），解构其结构、检索策略、章节、图表与写作风格，提炼借鉴清单。可选用 paper-lookup (K-Dense-AI) 做被引/施引网络分析，定位高质量标杆。Use when the user wants to learn from top reviews in a field, benchmark a review against top journals, study high-impact review article structure, or "find highly-cited reviews on X". ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-benchmark) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch-rest**: 写综述 Results / Discussion / Conclusion 三章 + references.bib，每节首尾有过渡句，图编号一致，discussion 不重复 results。Use when the user wants to write the results, discussion, and conclusion of a review, or export references in journal style. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch-rest) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch1**: 写综述 Introduction 章：漏斗式结构（broad → narrow → gap → aim），全部引用真实文献。Use when the user wants to write the introduction of a review article, draft the background section, or state the aim of a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch1) · ★1)
- **sci-review-ch2**: 写综述 Methods 章：检索策略、筛选、数据提取、合成方法、PRISMA 数字闭环、PRISMA 2020/GRADE/SWiM 引用。Use when the user wants to write the methods section of a review, document the search strategy, or build a PRISMA-aligned methods chapter. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-ch2) · ★1)
- **sci-review-cover-letter**: 写 Cover Letter（投稿信）+ Highlights + Graphical Abstract 文字版 + 推荐/排除审稿人，符合期刊字数与格式。Use when the user wants to write a cover letter to editor, draft highlights, or propose reviewers. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-cover-letter) · ★1)
- **sci-review-expert**: SCI 综述全流程总控。负责按 立项 → 检索 → 选题 → 对标 → 筛选 → Zotero → 精读 → 框架 → 配图 → 章节 → 选刊 → 规范 → 润色 → 授权 → 投稿信 → 自检 → 审稿回复 顺序编排 21 个子 skill，维护项目状态机、用户交互、每阶段门禁判定、异常回退。Use when the user wants to write a systematic review or narrative review for an SCI journal, says 帮我写综述 / 启动综述流程 / 综述全流程 / run review pipeline / SCI review workflow, or asks Codex to coordinate all review sub-skills. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-expert) · ★1)
- **sci-review-figure-license**: 对非自创且非开放许可的图发起授权申请，写邮件模板、跟踪回复、维护 license_status.csv。Use when the user wants to request figure permission, write a copyright permission email, or track image licenses for a manuscript. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-figure-license) · ★1)
- **sci-review-figures**: 制作综述所需图（PRISMA、概念图、时间线、证据汇总表、机制图），输出矢量+位图双版本并管理图注与授权状态。Use when the user wants to make PRISMA flow diagram, draw a conceptual figure, build an evidence summary table, or design figures for a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-figures) · ★1)
- **sci-review-framework**: 把素材组织成可写的章节大纲、故事线与配图计划，做逻辑自洽与重复检查。Use when the user wants to outline a review, build a review framework, design chapter structure, or plan figures for a review. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-framework) · ★1)
- **sci-review-fulltext**: 对每篇纳入文献做结构化精读，提取 8 要素笔记与横向证据表，标注可入综述的论点。Use when the user wants to extract data from PDFs, build an evidence table for a review, do structured reading notes, or align papers to a review framework. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-fulltext) · ★1)
- **sci-review-init**: 综述项目初始化。收集研究方向、用途、截止时间、目标期刊范围，建立工作目录结构与 project_meta.yaml / timeline.md / risk_register.md / project_state.json。Use when the user wants to start a SCI review, kick off a systematic review, or set up project meta and timeline. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-init) · ★1)
- **sci-review-journal-pick**: 用 LetPub / Elsevier Finder / Springer Suggester 找候选期刊，按 scope/IF/速度/费用/风险五维评分，推荐 3 主推 + 2 备选。Use when the user wants to pick a target journal for a review, compare IF and review speed, or shortlist journals in a field. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-journal-pick) · ★1)
- **sci-review-journal-rules**: 把锁定期刊的 Author Guidelines 逐条落到稿件上，输出 ≥30 项可勾选规则清单与差异处置。Use when the user wants to check journal submission guidelines, align a manuscript to a target journal, or build a compliance gap list. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-journal-rules) · ★1)
- **sci-review-polish**: 按目标期刊语言与风格精修稿件，统一术语与缩写、句法优化、查重控制，输出润色稿与 polish_log。Use when the user wants to polish a manuscript, run an iThenticate check, fix English writing, or unify terminology. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-polish) · ★1)
- **sci-review-rebuttal**: 应对大修/小修/拒稿转投的审稿意见，逐点回复、生成修订稿与改动定位表，保留 track changes。Use when the user wants to respond to reviewer comments, write a rebuttal letter, prepare a revised manuscript, or handle a major/minor revision. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-rebuttal) · ★1)
- **sci-review-screening**: 按 PRISMA 2020 流程做文献初筛（标题/摘要）与复筛（全文），双人筛选并算 kappa，输出 PRISMA 流程图与最终纳入清单。Use when the user wants to screen references by inclusion/exclusion criteria, build a PRISMA flow diagram, or do dual-reviewer screening. ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-screening) · ★1)
- **sci-review-search-builder**: 把研究问题转成 PubMed / Web of Science / Scopus / Embase / Cochrane 等数据库可复用的检索式，含概念块拆分、同义词/MeSH 补全、布尔组合与每库语法适配。Use when the user wants to build a literature search string, design a systematic search strategy, generate PubMed or WoS queries, or needs MeSH/Emtree terms. - **sci-review-search-runner**: 执行多库检索、合并去重、导出 RIS/CSV/BibTeX 元数据与摘要、记录 PRISMA 计数。优先调用可选的 paper-lookup (K-Dense-AI) 做跨库统一检索,否则 fallback 到 E-Utils + Crossref + Semantic Scholar。Use when the user wants to run a literature search, deduplicate references, export from PubMed/WoS/Scopus, build a PRISMA identification count, or "look up papers on X", "find DOI", "who cites this paper". ([centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline](https://github.com/centuryrain1980/sci-review-pipeline/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-review-search-runner) · ★1)
- **sci-review-submit**: 检查投稿系统所需全部材料、生成风险自检报告与 submission_package，跟踪投稿 ID 与状态。Use when the user wants a submission checklist, risk report for a manuscript, or to package files for a journal portal. - **sci-review-topic-gap**: 从检索结果中识别主题聚类、热度时间线、研究空白与候选选题。Use when the user wants to identify research gaps, find hot topics, cluster references, or pick a review topic with novelty scoring. - **sci-scientific-mindset**: Postura epistemica, desconstrucao de dogmas, debate cientifico critico, brainstorming dialetico, analise contrafactual e geracao de novos insights teoricos. Use quando o usuario quiser debater o estado da arte, desafiar consensos estabelecidos, explorar hipoteses radicais/contrafactuais, realizar deliberacao multi-perspectiva (advogado do diabo) ou construir matrizes de decisao epistemica sem tarefas operacionais rigidas. ([camillanapoles/nothing-phone2-recovery](https://github.com/camillanapoles/nothing-phone2-recovery/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-scientific-mindset))
- **sci-search**: Academic paper search and metrics analysis. Searches arXiv, PubMed, Web of Science, Springer Nature, Scopus, Semantic Scholar, and OpenAlex simultaneously with journal impact factor data. Triggers on requests to search for papers or find literature. ([ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write](https://github.com/ShZhao27208/Aut_Sci_Write/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-search) · ★225)
- **sci-search**: Поиск и верификация внешних фактов по первоисточникам: DOI, стандарты (ГОСТ/ISO/IEC), нормативы (НРБ/ICRP/IAEA), патенты, datasheet, ядерные данные. Обязателен ЛЮБОМУ агенту перед тем, как назвать число, формулу, норматив или ссылку — память модели источником НЕ является. Триггеры: «чему равен», «какой норматив/предел», «найди источник», «проверь ссылку», «действует ли ГОСТ». Один факт/число/ссылка — сюда; большой обзор → deep-research; своя библиотека дозиметрии → library; личные документы оператора → приватный RAG-скилл. ([VibeEngineering-LLC/agent-ops-doctrine](https://github.com/VibeEngineering-LLC/agent-ops-doctrine/tree/HEAD/skills/sci-search) · ★1)
- **sci-select**: Use when a user needs SCI/SCIE/ESCI/SSCI journal-submission help: evidence-backed candidate-journal discovery from a title, abstract, keywords, or manuscript text; public-metric and risk lookup for a known journal; or a pre-submission compliance check of a draft against a chosen journal's Author Guidelines and same-journal conventions. Also for Chinese requests such as 选刊、投稿期刊推荐、期刊查询、中科院分区、投稿前检查、期刊要求核对. This skill produces candidates, evidence, checks, and uncertainty; it does not judge research quality, predict acceptance, choose a uniquely best journal, draft prose, or simulate peer review. - **sci-tldr**: 색인(index.csv)에 논문마다 한국어 한 줄 요약(한줄요약 열)을 붙인다. "한 줄 요약 만들어줘", "한줄요약 붙여줘", "논문마다 요약 달아줘", "sci-tldr"에 사용하며 리버·김리버·김리버씨·댕댕아·댕댕이·김댕댕·김댕댕씨 호칭이 앞에 붙어도 적용한다. 수집·색인만 요청했다면 실행하지 않는다. 요약은 초록·본문 앞부분에 근거하고 숫자·화학식은 원문과 대조한다. - **SCI-writing-VIP**: 专属可持续自我迭代型生物医药 SCI 科研助手（代号 科研贾维斯；**领域不局限生信——生信/组学/药理/临床/免疫/动物模型/分子机制均纳入，见 §2.1 三层取材与 harvest-protocol §二**）。三层取材 + 双层节奏：日课七阶段（静默执行，每日 ≥74 篇归档 + **逐篇框架拆解** + 语言范式提取 + 仿写初稿 + 问题表述强度诊断 + 两轮修订 + 沉淀）；选材分周课主池（近 1 年 / 全 IF，保量）与顶刊 reserve 池（近十年 / IF&gt;20 / 三大顶刊为主，学“人类如何表述问题”）；周课三件套（领域前沿研究快报 Word——**只解读重要文献，按“采用什么方法/解决了什么问题/弥补什么空白”＋方法学体系 M1–M7 七层完整拆解，正文 ≤8 页、3–8 篇，不做文献计量堆砌** + 50 篇 IMRAD 架构扫面与 2 篇深拆 + 通用写作框架模板，热点固定近两年）。用于『每日文献训练』『论文框架拆解（构思/自洽/方法-问题匹配）』『分部写法（摘要 / 引言漏斗四步 / 结果 / 讨论 / 图注 / cover letter）』『语言范式/句式/连接词拆解』『问题表述强度』『证据强度—结论强度校准』『CNS首篇周课深拆』『标题/摘要/引言优先拆解』『逐句功能与figure panel拆解』『每日训练总纲与总纲评分』『事实、推断与猜测区… ([speetle/SCI-writing-VIP](https://github.com/speetle/SCI-writing-VIP/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)
